Kết quả chấm bài

Substantiated behaviors: Process execution (dl_lab.exe from explorer.exe), DNS query to update.labnet.local, HTTP GET /files/upd.bin, scheduled task creation (SystemHealthCheck), SHA256 hash matching (5D0E7A13C6B94F28E1A07D3B92C5F6084E1D7B3A60C9F25E8D41B7A3C02F9E64), timeline events (21:08:51–21:12:46). Defects: Missing registry key addition, missing file drop evidence, placeholder in network mode, unresolved evidence reference for User-Agent, incomplete timeline (event 5 missing), inferred persistence without evidence, missing environmental outcome (no logoff/logon test).

Kết luận hành vi\n\nTóm tắt chuỗi hành vi đã thực sự quan sát được.
